At last, we complete the proof by summing up (
4.18
), (
4.19
), (
4.1
), (
4.22
), (
4.1
) and (
4.1
).
∎

4.2.
L^{p}
-estimate and proof of Proposition
2.3
.

Lemma 4.5
.

For any
1<p<2
, it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{6}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{6}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\langle t\rangle\left[\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{p}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{6}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\quad+(\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{p}}+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{p}})\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad\times\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right].

Proof.

Applying
\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}
to
\eqref{eq:nonlinear part}_{2}
and taking the inner product of the resulting equation with
\left|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right|^{p-2}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}
, we find that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p}\frac{d}{dt}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{p}}^{p}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\Re\left(\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\left(u\cdot\nabla\theta\right)\Big|\left|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right|^{p-2}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right).

By Hölder inequality, we have

\displaystyle\left|\Re\left(\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\left(u\cdot\nabla\theta\right)\Big|\left|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right|^{p-2}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}(u\cdot\nabla\theta)\|_{L^{p}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{p}}^{p-1}.

and therefore

\frac{d}{dt}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{p}}\leq\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}(u\cdot\nabla\theta)\|_{L^{p}}.

Estimate of
u^{\mathrm{L}}\cdot\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{L}}
.

For the source term
u^{\mathrm{L}}\cdot\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{L}}
, one gets

\displaystyle\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\left(u^{\mathrm{L}}\cdot\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\right)\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\Delta^{-1}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{\frac{2p}{2-p}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}},

where we used

\displaystyle\nabla^{\perp}f\cdot\nabla g=\left(\begin{array}[]{c}-(\partial_{y}+t\partial_{x})f\\
\partial_{x}f\end{array}\right)\cdot\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\partial_{x}g\\
(\partial_{y}+t\partial_{x})g\end{array}\right)

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}f\|_{L^{\frac{2p}{2-p}}}\lesssim\|\langle k,\xi+kt\rangle^{3}\hat{f}_{k}(\xi)\|_{L_{k,\xi}^{\frac{2p}{3p-2}}}\\
&\lesssim\|\langle k,\xi+kt\rangle^{5}\hat{f}_{k}(\xi)\|_{L_{k,\xi}^{2}}\|\langle k,\xi+kt\rangle^{-2}\|_{L_{k,\xi}^{p^{\prime}}}\lesssim\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}f\|_{L^{2}},\end{aligned}

(4.36)

for
1<p<2
and some sufficiently smooth
f
and
g
.

Estimate of
u^{\mathrm{NL}}\cdot\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{L}}
and
u\cdot\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}
.

Using (
4.10
) and the following classical estimate of Biot-Savart law:

\|\nabla^{\perp}(-\Delta)^{-1}f\|_{L^{\frac{2p}{2-p}}}\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{p}},

(4.37)

we have that

\displaystyle\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\left(u^{\mathrm{NL}}\cdot\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{L}}+u\cdot\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}u^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\frac{2p}{2-p}}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}u\|_{L^{\frac{2p}{2-p}}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle t\rangle\left[\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{p}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad+(\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{p}}+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{p}})\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right].

Adding all inequalities above together completes the proof.
∎

Similarly, for
\omega
and
\partial_{x}\theta
, we can use the analogous methods and state the estimates as follows.

Lemma 4.6
.

For any
1<p<2
, if the initial data satisfies (
1.7
), then one has

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{6}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\partial_{x}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle+\langle t\rangle\left[\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{p}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{6}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\quad\quad+\left(\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{p}}+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{p}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right].

Lemma 4.7
.

For any
1<p<2
, if the initial data satisfies (
1.7
), then one has

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\partial_{x}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{6}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{6}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\langle t\rangle\left[\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{6}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad\times\left(\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{p}}+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\quad\quad+(\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{p}}+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{6}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\quad\qquad+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{p}})\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad\times\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\langle\partial_{x}\rangle\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right].

Proof.

By
\eqref{eq:nonlinear part}_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p}\frac{d}{dt}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\partial_{x}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{p}}^{p}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\Re\left(\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\partial_{x}\left(u\cdot\nabla\theta\right)\Big|\left|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\partial_{x}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right|^{p-2}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\partial_{x}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right),

which implies

\frac{d}{dt}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\partial_{x}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{p}}\lesssim\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\partial_{x}(u\cdot\nabla\theta)\|_{L^{p}}.

Estimate of
u\cdot\nabla\partial_{x}\theta
.
Following a similar reasoning to that in Lemma
4.5
, with appropriate adjustments, we arrive at

\displaystyle\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\left(u\cdot\nabla\partial_{x}\theta\right)\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{6}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{6}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\langle t\rangle\left[\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{p}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{6}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\quad+(\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{p}}+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{p}})\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad\times\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\partial_{x}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right].

Estimate of
\partial_{x}u^{\mathrm{L}}\cdot\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{L}}
.

By (
4.2
) and (
4.36
), one has

\displaystyle\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\left(\partial_{x}u^{\mathrm{L}}\cdot\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\right)\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\Delta^{-1}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{\frac{2p}{2-p}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{6}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}.

Estimate of
\partial_{x}u^{\mathrm{NL}}\cdot\nabla\theta
and
\partial_{x}u^{\mathrm{L}}\cdot\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}
.

Thank to (
4.36
), (
4.37
), (
4.10
) and (
4.21
), we have

\displaystyle\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\left(\partial_{x}u^{\mathrm{NL}}\cdot\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{L}}+\partial_{x}u^{\mathrm{L}}\cdot\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\partial_{x}u^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{\frac{2p}{2-p}}}

\displaystyle+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\partial_{x}u^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{\frac{2p}{2-p}}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle t\rangle\left(\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{p}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

and

\displaystyle\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\left(\partial_{x}u^{\mathrm{NL}}\cdot\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle\partial_{x}u^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\frac{2p}{2-p}}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\partial_{x}u^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\frac{2p}{2-p}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle t\rangle(\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{p}}+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}})\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}.

In all, the proof is completed.
∎

To conclude this section, we present the proof of Proposition
2.3
.

Proof of Proposition
2.3
.

For
t\leq T_{0}=\nu^{-\frac{1}{6}}
, we get by Lemma
4.3
that

\displaystyle\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu^{\frac{2}{3}+2\delta}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\partial_{x}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\delta}{6}}(\nu^{\frac{1}{3}+\delta}+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}})

\displaystyle\times(\|\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{p}}+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}),

by Lemma
4.2
that

\displaystyle\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\nu\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\left[\nu^{1+3\delta}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad+\nu^{\frac{1}{3}+\frac{11}{6}\delta}(\|\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{p}}+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}})\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\quad+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\delta}{6}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}(\nu^{\frac{1}{3}+\delta}+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}})\right],

by Lemma
4.4
that

\displaystyle\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\partial_{x}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu^{\frac{1}{3}+\delta}\left(\nu^{\frac{2}{3}+2\delta}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\delta}{6}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\partial_{x}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\quad+\nu^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\nu^{\frac{1}{3}+\frac{11}{6}\delta}\left(\|\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{p}}+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\left(\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\delta}{6}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\partial_{x}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad\quad+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{12}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\right),

by Lemma
4.6
that

\displaystyle\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu^{\frac{2}{3}+2\delta}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\partial_{x}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{p}}

\displaystyle+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\left[\nu^{\frac{1}{3}+\delta}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{p}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad+(\nu^{\frac{1}{3}+\delta}+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{p}})\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle+\nu^{-\frac{1}{6}}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\partial_{x}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{p}},

by Lemma
4.5
that

\displaystyle\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu^{1+3\delta}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\left[\nu^{\frac{2}{3}+2\delta}\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{p}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad\quad+(\nu^{\frac{1}{3}+\delta}+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{p}})\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\right],

and by Lemma
4.7
that

\displaystyle\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\partial_{x}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu^{1+3\delta}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\left[\nu^{\frac{2}{3}+2\delta}(\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{p}}+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}})\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad+(\nu^{\frac{1}{3}+\delta}+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}+\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{p}})\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad\quad\quad\times\|\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\langle\partial_{x}\rangle\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\right].

By bootstrap arguments, there exist a small enough
0<\nu_{1}<1
such that if
0<\nu<\nu_{1}
, (
2.5
) holds. The proof is completed.
∎

5.
Estimate of nonlinear part in long time scale
t\geq T_{0}=\nu^{-\frac{1}{6}}

In the last section, we will do energy estimates with the multiplier
\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}
and the Fourier multiplier
\mathcal{M}
we construct in Section
2
. The multiplier
\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}
helps to handle singularities, while the multiplier
\mathcal{M}
is devoted to extracting the stabilizing mechanisms and dealing with echo cascades.

5.1.
Estimate of
\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}

Proposition 5.1
.

There exist
\frac{1-\delta}{2}<\epsilon<\frac{1}{2}
,
0<\kappa<\frac{2\epsilon}{1-\delta}-1
and
c<c(\delta,\epsilon)
small enough such that if the initial data
(w_{in},\theta_{in})
satisfies (
1.7
), then for any
T\geq T_{0}=\nu^{-\frac{1}{6}}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\nu\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon\left(t,D_{x},D_{y}\right)}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}(T_{0})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\nu^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\nu^{\frac{1}{4}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt\times(\nu^{\frac{1}{12}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt+\nu^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle+(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}})

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\delta}{6}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}\|e^{c_{0}\nu\left|D_{x}\right|^{2}t^{3}}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}(L^{1}\cap L^{2})}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt\times(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}+\nu^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}})

\displaystyle+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}.

Remark 5.2
.

For the choice of the parameters such as
\epsilon,\kappa
and
\alpha
which appears below, one can find them in
[
38
]
.

Proof.

Taking the inner product of
\eqref{eq:nonlinear part}_{2}
with
\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{2}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}
, we infer that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|\sqrt{\mathcal{M}}

\displaystyle e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\sqrt{\mathcal{M}}e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\nu\|\sqrt{\mathcal{M}}e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left(-\partial_{t}+k\partial_{\xi}\right)\mathcal{M}(k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{2}{3}}\left|\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\right|^{2}dkd\xi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\Re\left((u^{\mathrm{L}}+u^{\mathrm{NL}})\cdot\nabla(\theta^{\mathrm{L}}+\theta^{\mathrm{NL}})\big|\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{2}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right).

Thanks to (
2.2
), (
2.3
) and (
2.1
), we use the fact that
\lambda(k)\leq|k|^{\frac{2}{3}}
to deduce that there exist some
0<c<C(C_{\kappa})
such that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|\sqrt{\mathcal{M}}e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\nu\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}}{16}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon\left(t,D_{x},D_{y}\right)}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left|\Re\left((u^{\mathrm{L}}+u^{\mathrm{NL}})\cdot\nabla(\theta^{\mathrm{L}}+\theta^{\mathrm{NL}})\big|\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{2}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\right|.

Since

\displaystyle(fg|\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{2}{3}}h)

\displaystyle\lesssim\big((1+|k|^{2})^{\frac{1}{6}}\widehat{fg}|(1+|k|^{2})^{\frac{1}{6}}\hat{h}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}|k-l|^{\frac{1}{3}}|\hat{f}(k-l)\hat{g}(l)|(1+|k|^{2})^{\frac{1}{6}}|\hat{h}(k)|dkdl

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(1+|l|^{2})^{\frac{1}{6}}|\hat{f}(k-l)\hat{g}(l)|(1+|k|^{2})^{\frac{1}{6}}|\hat{h}(k)|dkdl

holds for some
f,g
and
h
, we formally decompose
\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\big[(u^{\mathrm{L}}+u^{\mathrm{NL}})\cdot\nabla(\theta^{\mathrm{L}}+\theta^{\mathrm{NL}})\big]
into eight terms and proceed to estimate them one by one, which does not affect the upper bound estimates above.

Step I: Estimate of
u^{\mathrm{L}}\cdot\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}
.

Applying the Fourier transformation, there holds that

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\left|\left(u^{\mathrm{L}}\cdot\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\big|\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(D_{x})t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\right|

(5.1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hskip 18.49988pt\times\frac{\eta(k-l)-l(\xi-\eta)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|.

We then consider two cases for the frequency
k
: high and low frequencies.

\bullet
Case 1:
|k|\geq 1
.
Using (
4.6
), (
4.7
) and

\langle k\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}\lesssim\langle l,\eta+lt\rangle\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle,

we get that

\displaystyle\left|\int_{|k|\geq 1}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\right.

(5.2)

\displaystyle\left.\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\times\frac{\eta(k-l)-l(\xi-\eta)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\|_{L_{k,\xi}^{2}}\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l,\eta+lt\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{4}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{-2}\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{4}\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}.

\bullet
Case 2:
|k|<1
. By (
4.6
), (
4.7
) and

\langle k\rangle\lesssim\langle l,\eta+lt\rangle\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle,

we have that

\displaystyle\left|\int_{|k|<1}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\times\frac{\eta(k-l)-l(\xi-\eta)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}\|_{L_{k}^{2}([-1,1])}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\|_{L_{k,\xi}^{2}}\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l,\eta+lt\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{4}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{-1}\|_{L_{\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{3}\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}.

(5.3)

Hence, (
5.1
), (
5.2
) and (
5.1
) gives

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\left|\left(u^{\mathrm{L}}\cdot\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\big|\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\right|

(5.4)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}.

Step II: Estimate of
|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}u^{\mathrm{L}}\cdot\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{L}}
.

Write

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\left|\left(|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}u^{\mathrm{L}}\cdot\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\big|D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}|k|^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)|l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hskip 9.24994pt\times\frac{\eta(k-l)-l(\xi-\eta)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|.

(
4.7
) is substituted by

\frac{|l|^{\frac{1}{3}}}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\langle l,\eta+lt\rangle^{2}}{|l|^{\frac{11}{3}}\langle t\rangle^{2}}\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-2}\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{4}\langle l,\eta+lt\rangle^{2}.

Then, similarly as (
5.4
), we have

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\left|\left(|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}u^{\mathrm{L}}\cdot\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\left\lvert\,\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right.\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}.

Step III: Estimate of
u^{\mathrm{L}}\cdot\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}
.
Due to the different decay behaviors of
u_{1}^{\mathrm{L}}\partial_{x}
and
u_{2}^{\mathrm{L}}\partial_{y}
, we consider them separately.

Step III.1: Estimate of
u_{2}^{\mathrm{L}}\partial_{y}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}
.

The Fourier transform shows that

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\left|\left(u_{2}^{\mathrm{L}}\partial_{y}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\left\lvert\,\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right.\right)\right|

(5.5)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{l(\xi-\eta)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|.

\bullet
Case 1:
|k|\geq 1
. From
\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle k\rangle\lesssim\langle k-l\rangle\langle l,\eta+lt\rangle
and

l(|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2})^{-1}\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-2}\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{3}\langle l,\eta+lt\rangle^{2},

(5.6)

there holds

\displaystyle\left|\int_{|k|\geq 1}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{l(\xi-\eta)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|

(5.7)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\|_{L_{k,\xi}^{2}}\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|\langle l,\eta+lt\rangle^{-2}\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l,\eta+lt\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{3}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l\rangle(\xi-\eta)\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{3}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\partial_{y}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}.

\bullet
Case 2:
|k|<1
. According to (
5.6
) and
\langle k\rangle^{2}\lesssim 1
, we arrive

\displaystyle\left|\int_{|k|<1}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{l(\xi-\eta)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|

(5.8)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}\|_{L_{k}^{2}([-1,1])}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\|_{L_{k,\xi}^{2}}\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l,\eta+lt\rangle^{3}\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{3}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|\langle\eta+lt\rangle^{-1}\|_{L_{\eta}^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}|\xi-\eta|\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{3}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\partial_{y}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}.

Adding (
5.5
), (
5.7
) and (
5.8
) together, we infer that

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\left|\left(u_{2}^{\mathrm{L}}\partial_{y}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\big|\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\right|

(5.9)

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{3}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\partial_{y}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}.

Step III.2: Estimate of
u_{1}^{\mathrm{L}}\partial_{x}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}
.

Note that

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\left|\left(u_{1}^{\mathrm{L}}\partial_{x}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\big|\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\right|

(5.10)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{\eta(k-l)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|.

We decompose the integration domain into two parts: the
k
-near-diagonal region
, where
|k-l|\leq 2|k|
, and the
k
-off-diagonal region
, where
|k-l|>2|k|
.

\bullet
Case 1:
|k-l|\leq 2|k|
. Using
\langle k\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}\lesssim\langle k-l\rangle\langle l,\eta+lt\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{\epsilon}
,
|k-l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\lesssim|k|^{\frac{1}{3}}
and

\eta(|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2})^{-1}\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-1}\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{2}\langle l,\eta+lt\rangle,

(5.11)

one has

\displaystyle\left|\int_{|k-l|\leq 2|k|}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{\eta(k-l)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}|k|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\|_{L_{k,\xi}^{2}}\langle t\rangle^{-1}\|\langle l,\eta+lt\rangle^{-2}\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l,\eta+lt\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{2}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{\epsilon}|k-l|^{\frac{2}{3}}\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}|k|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\|_{L_{k,\xi}^{2}}\langle t\rangle^{-1}\|\langle l,\eta+lt\rangle^{-2}\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l,\eta+lt\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{2}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{\epsilon}|k-l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{\epsilon}|k-l|\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-1}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(5.12)

\bullet
Case 2:
2|k|<|k-l|
. In this case, it follows from
|l|\sim|k-l|
that
\langle k\rangle\lesssim\langle l\rangle
. By
\langle k\rangle^{2}\lesssim\langle l,\eta+lt\rangle\langle k-l\rangle
,
|k-l|\lesssim\langle l,\eta+lt\rangle|k-l|^{\frac{1}{3}}
and (
5.11
), we have

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\left|\int_{2|k|<|k-l|}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{\eta(k-l)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|

(5.13)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\langle k\rangle^{-1}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}\|_{L_{k}^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\|_{L_{k,\xi}^{2}}\langle t\rangle^{-1}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l,\eta+lt\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{2}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|\langle\eta+lt\rangle^{-1}\|_{L_{\eta}^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l\rangle|k-l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-1}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}.

It follows from (
5.10
), (
5.1
) and (
5.13
) that

\displaystyle\left|\left(u_{1}^{\mathrm{L}}\partial_{x}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\big|\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-1}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\times(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}),

which along with (
5.9
) implies

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\left|\left(u^{\mathrm{L}}\cdot\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\big|\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\right|

(5.14)

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{3}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\partial_{y}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\langle t\rangle^{-1}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\times(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}).

Step IV: Estimate of
|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}u^{\mathrm{L}}\cdot\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}
.

Writing (
5.6
) and (
5.11
) as
|l|^{\frac{4}{3}}(|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2})^{-1}\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-2}\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{3}\langle l,\eta+lt\rangle^{2}
and
|l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\eta(|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2})^{-1}\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-1}\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{2}\langle l,\eta+lt\rangle
, respectively,
we infer similarly as (
5.14
) that

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\left|\left(|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}u^{\mathrm{L}}\cdot\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\big|\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{3}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\partial_{y}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\langle t\rangle^{-1}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{2}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right).

Step V: Estimate of
u^{\mathrm{NL}}\cdot\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}
.
For this term, we decompose it into two parts as follows:

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\left|\left(u^{\mathrm{NL}}\cdot\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\big|\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\right|

(5.15)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\times\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{\eta(k-l)-l(\xi-\eta)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle I_{1}+I_{2},

where
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
are defined by

\begin{gathered}I_{1}:=\bigg|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{\eta(k-l)-l(\xi-\eta+t(k-l))}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\\
\times\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\bigg|,\\
I_{2}:=\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{tl(k-l)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|.\end{gathered}

Step V.1: Estimate of
I_{1}
.

We classify the analysis into three cases according to the magnitude of the frequency
k
and the proximity between
k
and
l
.

\bullet
Case 1:
|k-l|\leq 2|l|
and
|k|>1
. It follows from
\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\lesssim 1
,

\displaystyle\langle k\rangle\lesssim\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle\langle l\rangle,

(5.16)

\displaystyle|k-l|+|\xi-\eta+t(k-l)|\leq\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle,

(5.17)

\displaystyle|l|^{-1}\lesssim|k-l|^{-1}\lesssim\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{2}|k-l|^{\frac{1}{3}},

(5.18)

that

\displaystyle\left|\int_{|k-l|\leq 2|l|,\,|k|>1}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{\eta(k-l)-l(\xi-\eta+t(k-l))}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\times\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\|_{L_{k,\xi}^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l\rangle\frac{l}{|l|+|\eta|}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}\|\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{-2}\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{2}|k-l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}.

(5.19)

\bullet
Case 2:
|k-l|\leq 2|l|
and
|k|\leq 1
. Using (
5.16
), (
5.17
) and (
5.18
) again, we have

\displaystyle\left|\int_{|k-l|\leq 2|l|,|k|\leq 1}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{\eta(k-l)-l(\xi-\eta+t(k-l))}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\times\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}\|_{L_{k}^{2}([-1,1])}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\|_{L_{k,\xi}^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l\rangle\frac{l}{|l|+|\eta|}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|\langle\xi-\eta+t(k-l)\rangle^{-1}\|_{L_{\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{3}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{2}|k-l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}.

(5.20)

\bullet
Case 3:
|k-l|>2|l|
. In this
l
-near-diagonal region
, it follows that
|k|\sim|k-l|
. Then, by (
5.17
),
\langle k\rangle\lesssim\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle
,
\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\lesssim\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\lesssim|k|^{\frac{1}{3}}|k-l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{2}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\lvert\,\int_{2|l|<|k-l|}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{\eta(k-l)-l(\xi-\eta+t(k-l))}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\times\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\mid

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle|k|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\|_{L_{k,\xi}^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l\rangle\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{\epsilon}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}\|\langle l\rangle^{-1}\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{-\epsilon}\|\frac{1}{|l|+|\eta|}\|_{L_{\eta}^{2}}\|_{L_{l}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{2}|k-l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}},

(5.21)

where we used that for
\epsilon>0
,

\|\langle l\rangle^{-1}\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{-\epsilon}\|\frac{1}{|l|+|\eta|}\|_{L_{\eta}^{2}}\|_{L_{l}^{2}}\lesssim\|\langle l\rangle^{-1}\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{-\epsilon}|l|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L_{l}^{2}}\lesssim 1.

Combining (
5.1
), (
5.1
) and (
5.1
), it holds that

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right.

(5.22)

\displaystyle\left.\hskip 18.49988pt+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

Step V.2: Estimate of
I_{2}
.

For the real reaction term
I_{2}
, we will employ the multiplier
\mathcal{M}_{3}
to address this challenge.

\bullet
Case 1:
|k-l|\leq 2|k|
. From (
5.16
) and

\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}\lesssim\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{\epsilon}\lesssim|k|^{\frac{1}{3}\delta}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle,

there holds that

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\left|\int_{|k-l|\leq 2|k|}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{tl(k-l)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|

(5.23)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l\rangle\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{\epsilon}\frac{l}{\sqrt{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|te^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{3}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}|k|^{\frac{2}{3}\delta}\frac{\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{-2\epsilon}}{\left(|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}\right)\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{2}}\left|\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\right|^{2}dkdld\xi d\eta\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Then, on the one hand, it follows that

\displaystyle\|te^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{3}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}|k-l|^{\frac{2}{3}}te^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}|k-l|^{\frac{2}{3}}t}\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{3}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{2}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c_{0}\nu|k-l|^{2}t^{3}}\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{3}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{2}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

On the other hand, it follows from the convolution properties of the Poisson kernel (e.g., see (2-12) in
[
56
]
) and the trick in
[
38
]
that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{-2\epsilon}}{\left(|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}\right)\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{2}}d\eta dl

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}}\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{-2\epsilon}\frac{1}{|l|}\frac{1+|l|+|k-l|}{(1+|k-l|)\left(\langle l,k-l\rangle^{2}+|\xi+t(k-l)|^{2}\right)}dl

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}}\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{-\frac{1}{3}-\kappa}\frac{1}{|l|^{\frac{1}{3}}}\frac{1+|l|+|k-l|}{(1+|k-l|)\left(\langle l,k-l\rangle^{2}+|\xi+t(k-l)|^{2}\right)}dl\right)^{1-\delta}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}}\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{-\alpha}\frac{1}{|l|}\langle l\rangle^{-1}dl\right)^{\delta}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Upsilon(t,k,\xi)^{1-\delta},

where
\alpha=\frac{2\epsilon-(1-\delta)(1+\kappa)}{\delta}
satisfies

(1-\delta)(-1-\kappa)+\delta(-\alpha)=-2\epsilon

and one can choose
0<\kappa<\frac{2\epsilon}{1-\delta}-1
so that
\alpha>0
, when
\epsilon>\frac{1-\delta}{2}
. According to the two estimates above, we arrive at

\displaystyle\left|\int_{|k-l|\leq 2|k|}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{tl(k-l)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l\rangle\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{\epsilon}\frac{l}{\sqrt{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\times\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c_{0}\nu|k-l|^{2}t^{3}}\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{3}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{2}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\epsilon-\eta}^{2}}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}|k|^{\frac{\delta}{3}}\Upsilon(t,k,\xi)^{\frac{1-\delta}{2}}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\|_{L_{k,\epsilon}^{2}}\\
&\lesssim\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c_{0}\nu\left|D_{x}\right|^{2}t^{3}}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\times\left(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right)^{\delta}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{1-\delta}.\end{aligned}

\bullet
Case 2:
|k-l|>2|k|
. In such a case, thanks to

\displaystyle\langle k\rangle^{2}\lesssim\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle\langle l\rangle,

(5.24)

\displaystyle 1\lesssim\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{-\frac{1+\kappa}{2}+\epsilon},

(5.25)

it holds

\displaystyle\left|\int_{2|k|<|k-l|}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\times\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{tl(k-l)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\langle k\rangle^{-1}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}\|_{L_{k}^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l\rangle\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{\epsilon}\frac{l}{\sqrt{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}\|\langle\xi-\eta+t(k-l)\rangle^{-1}\|_{L_{\xi}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|te^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}e^{2c\nu^{3}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\frac{\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{-1-\kappa}}{\left(|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}\right)\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{2}}\left|\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\right|^{2}dkdld\xi d\eta\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l\rangle\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{\epsilon}\frac{l}{\sqrt{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon(t,k,\xi)}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\|_{L_{k,\epsilon}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c_{0}\nu|k-l|^{2}t^{3}}\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{2}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c_{0}\nu\left|D_{x}\right|^{2}t^{3}}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}.

Combining the above two cases, we deduce that

\displaystyle I_{2}\lesssim\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\delta}{6}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c_{0}\nu\left|D_{x}\right|^{2}t^{3}}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{1}\cap L^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}+\nu^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right),

which, combined (
5.22
) and (
