), gives that

\displaystyle\left|\left(u^{\mathrm{NL}}\cdot\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\big|\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hskip 9.24994pt+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\delta}{6}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c_{0}\nu\left|D_{x}\right|^{2}t^{3}}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{1}\cap L^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}+\nu^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right).

Step VI: Estimate of
|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}u^{\mathrm{NL}}\cdot\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{L}}
.
We divide this term into two parts as follows:

\displaystyle\left|\left(|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}u^{\mathrm{NL}}\cdot\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\big|\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}|k|^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)|l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{\eta(k-l)-l(\xi-\eta)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle I_{3}+I_{4},

where

\begin{gathered}I_{3}:=\left\lvert\,\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}|k|^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)|l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{\eta(k-l)-l(\xi-\eta+t(k-l))}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\right.\\
\times\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\mid\\
I_{4}:=\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}|k|^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)|l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{tl(k-l)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|.\end{gathered}

StepVI.1: Estimate of
I_{3}
.

For
I_{3}
, it is similar as
I_{1}
.

\bullet
Case 1:
|k-l|\leq 2|l|
. For
|k|>1
, it follows from (
5.16
),
\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\lesssim 1
and
|l|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\lesssim|k-l|^{-\frac{2}{3}}\lesssim\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{2}|k-l|^{\frac{2}{3}}
that

\displaystyle\left|\int_{|k-l|\leq 2|l|,\,|k|>1}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}|k|^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)|l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{\eta(k-l)-l(\xi-\eta+t(k-l))}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\times\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle|k|^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\|_{L_{k,\xi}^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l\rangle\frac{l}{|l|+|\eta|}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}\|\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{-2}\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{2}|k-l|^{\frac{2}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{2}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

(5.26)

and similarly for
|k|\leq 1
,

\displaystyle\left\lvert\,\int_{|k-l|\leq 2|l|,|k|\leq 1}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}|k|^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)|l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{\eta(k-l)-l(\xi-\eta+t(k-l))}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\times\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\mid

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}\|_{L_{k}^{2}([-1,1])}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}|k|^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\|_{L_{k,\xi}^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l\rangle\frac{l}{|l|+|\eta|}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|\langle\xi-\eta+t(k-l)\rangle^{-1}\|_{L_{\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{3}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{2}|k-l|^{\frac{2}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{2}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}.

(5.27)

\bullet
Case 2:
|k-l|>2|l|
. From
\langle k\rangle\lesssim\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle
,

|l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\lesssim|k-l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\lesssim|k|^{\frac{1}{3}}|k-l|^{\frac{2}{3}}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{2}

and (
5.17
), we have

\displaystyle\left\lvert\,\int_{2|l|<|k-l|}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}|k|^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)|l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{\eta(k-l)-l(\xi-\eta+t(k-l))}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\times\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\mid

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle|k|^{\frac{2}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\|_{L_{k,\xi}^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l\rangle\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{\epsilon}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}\|\langle l\rangle^{-1}\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{-\epsilon}\|\frac{1}{|l|+|\eta|}\|_{L_{\eta}^{2}}\|_{L_{l}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{2}|k-l|^{\frac{2}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{2}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{2}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{2}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}.

(5.28)

Combining (
5.1
), (
5.1
) with (
5.1
), we get that

\displaystyle I_{3}\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right.

(5.29)

\displaystyle\left.\hskip 9.24994pt+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{2}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{2}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{2}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}.

StepVI.2: Estimate of
I_{4}
.

Now, we turn to
I_{4}
.

\bullet
Case 1:
|k-l|\leq 2|k|
. From (
5.16
) and
\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}\lesssim\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{\epsilon}\lesssim\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle
, it holds that

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\left|\int_{|k-l|\leq 2|k|}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}|k|^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)|l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{tl(k-l)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|

(5.30)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l\rangle\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{\epsilon}\frac{l}{\sqrt{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|te^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{3}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\frac{\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{-\frac{1}{3}-\kappa}|l|^{\frac{2}{3}}}{\left(|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}\right)\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{2}}\left||k|^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\right|^{2}dkdld\xi d\eta\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Hence, noting that

\displaystyle\|te^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{3}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c_{0}\nu|k-l|^{2}t^{3}}\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{3}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{2}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

and

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{-\frac{1}{3}-\kappa}|l|^{\frac{2}{3}}}{\left(|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}\right)\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{2}}d\eta dl

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{-\frac{1}{3}-\kappa}\frac{1}{|l|^{\frac{1}{3}}}\frac{1+|l|+|k-l|}{(1+|k-l|)\left(\langle l,k-l\rangle^{2}+|\xi+t(k-l)|^{2}\right)}dl\lesssim\Upsilon(t,k,\xi),

we deduce that

\displaystyle\left|\int_{|k-l|\leq 2|k|}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}|k|^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)|l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{tl(k-l)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c_{0}\nu\left|D_{x}\right|^{2}t^{3}}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{3}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}.

\bullet
Case 2:
2|k|<|k-l|
. (
5.24
), (
5.25
) and
|l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\lesssim|k-l|^{\frac{1}{3}}
implies

\displaystyle\left|\int_{2|k|<|k-l|}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}|k|^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)|l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{tl(k-l)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\langle k\rangle^{-1}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}\|_{L_{k}^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l\rangle\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{\epsilon}\frac{l}{\sqrt{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}\|\langle\xi-\eta+t(k-l)\rangle^{-1}\|_{L_{\xi}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|te^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle|k-l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}e^{2c\nu^{3}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\frac{\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{-1-\kappa}}{\left(|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}\right)\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{2}}\left||k|^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\right|^{2}dkdld\xi d\eta\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l\rangle\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{\epsilon}\frac{l}{\sqrt{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c_{0}\nu|k-l|^{2}t^{3}}\langle k-l,\xi-\eta+(k-l)t\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{2}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{L}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon(t,k,\xi)}|k|^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\|_{L_{k,\epsilon}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c_{0}\nu\left|D_{x}\right|^{2}t^{3}}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}.

Adding the above two cases together, we get that

\displaystyle I_{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\delta}{6}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c_{0}\nu\left|D_{x}\right|^{2}t^{3}}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{1}\cap L^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}+\nu^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{2}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right),

which along with (
5.29
) implies

\displaystyle\left|\left(|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}u^{\mathrm{NL}}\cdot\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\left\lvert\,\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right.\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hskip 9.24994pt+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{2}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{2}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{2}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\delta}{6}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c_{0}\nu\left|D_{x}\right|^{2}t^{3}}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{1}\cap L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\left(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}+\nu^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{2}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right).

Step VII: Estimate of
u^{\mathrm{NL}}\cdot\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}
.
Due to the different decay behaviors of
u_{1}^{\mathrm{NL}}\partial_{x}
and
u_{2}^{\mathrm{NL}}\partial_{y}
, our consideration is divided into two parts as follows

\displaystyle\left|\left(u^{\mathrm{NL}}\cdot\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\big|\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hskip 9.24994pt\times\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{\eta(k-l)-l(\xi-\eta)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle I_{5}+I_{6},

where

\begin{gathered}I_{5}:=\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{\eta(k-l)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\right.\\
\left.\times\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|\\
I_{6}:=\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{l(\xi-\eta)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|.\end{gathered}

Step VII.1: Estimate of
I_{5}
.

For
I_{5}
, we divide the integration domain into three parts according to the relationship between
|k|
and
|k-l|
, namely the near-resonant region (
\frac{|k-l|}{2}\leq|k|\leq 2|k-l|
), the high–low interaction region (
|k|>2|k-l|
), and the low–high interaction region (
2|k|<|k-l|
).

\bullet
Case 1:
\frac{|k-l|}{2}\leq|k|\leq 2|k-l|
. Using
\langle k\rangle\lesssim\langle k-l\rangle
and
\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}\lesssim\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{\epsilon}
, we infer that

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\frac{|k-l|}{2}\leq|k|\leq 2|k-l|}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{\eta(k-l)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\times\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}|k|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\|_{L_{k,\xi}^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l\rangle\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{\epsilon}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}\|\langle l\rangle^{-1}\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{-\epsilon}\|\frac{1}{|l|+|\eta|}\|_{L_{\eta}^{2}}\|_{L_{l}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{\epsilon}|k-l|^{\frac{2}{3}}\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(5.31)

\bullet
Case 2:
|k|>2|k-l|
. By
|k|\sim|l|
and
|k-l|\lesssim|k|\lesssim|l|
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\left|\int_{|k|>2|k-l|}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{\eta(k-l)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\times\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle k\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\|_{L_{k,\xi}^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l\rangle\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{\epsilon}\frac{l\eta}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|\langle k-l\rangle^{-1}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{-\epsilon}\|\frac{1}{|k-l|+|\xi-\eta|}\|_{L_{\xi-\eta}^{2}}\|_{L_{k-l}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{|k-l|^{2}+|\xi-\eta|^{2}}\langle k-l\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}.

(5.32)

\bullet
Case 3:
2|k|<|k-l|
. From
\langle k\rangle^{2}\lesssim\langle l\rangle\langle k-l\rangle
and

1\lesssim\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}|k-l|^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left(1+\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}-2\epsilon}\right)|k-l|^{\frac{1}{2}},

we get

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{\epsilon}|\xi-\eta|\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{y}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}},

where we use that
|l|^{\frac{1}{2}}\|(|l|+|\eta|)^{-1}\|_{L_{\eta}^{2}}\lesssim 1
.

\bullet
Case 3:
2|k|<|k-l|
. From
\langle k\rangle^{2}\lesssim\langle l\rangle\langle k-l\rangle
,
|k-l|^{\frac{1}{2}}|l|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim 1
,
|l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\sim|k-l|^{\frac{1}{3}}
and (
5.37
), we get that

\displaystyle\bigg|\int_{2|k|<|k-l|}\mathcal{M}(t,k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}|k|^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)|l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{l(\xi-\eta)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi-\eta)dkdld\xi d\eta\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}|k|^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi)\|_{L_{k,\xi}^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(l)t}\langle l\rangle\langle\frac{1}{l}\rangle^{\epsilon}\frac{|l|(|l|+|\eta|)}{|l|^{2}+|\eta|^{2}}\hat{\omega}_{l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\eta)\|_{L_{l,\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|\langle k\rangle^{-1}(\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\epsilon}+\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}-\epsilon})|k-l|^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\frac{1}{|l|+|\eta|}\|_{L_{\eta}^{2}}\|_{L_{k}^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k-l)t}\langle k-l\rangle\langle\frac{1}{k-l}\rangle^{\epsilon}|\xi-\eta||k-l|^{\frac{1}{3}}\hat{\theta}_{k-l}^{\mathrm{NL}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L_{k-l,\xi-\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{y}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}.

Combining the estimates of
I_{7}
and
I_{8}
shows that

\displaystyle\left|\left(|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}u^{\mathrm{NL}}\cdot\nabla\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\big|\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{2}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}.

Step IX: Collecting the estimates.
In summary, we have

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|\sqrt{\mathcal{M}}e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\nu\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\frac{\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}}{16}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon\left(t,D_{x},D_{y}\right)}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt\times\left(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\langle t\rangle^{-1}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt\times\left(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hskip 17.00024pt+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hskip 8.50012pt+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\delta}{6}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c_{0}\nu\left|D_{x}\right|^{2}t^{3}}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{1}\cap L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt\times\left(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}+\nu^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}.

Integrating the inequality above over
[T_{0},T]
and using

\displaystyle\|\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{1}}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{12}}\|\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}}+\nu^{\frac{1}{12}}\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}}+\|\langle t\rangle^{-1}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}}\leq\nu^{\frac{1}{6}},

(5.39)

we complete the proof.
∎

Estimate of

2.5

The estimate for

Proposition 5.3
.

Let
\frac{1-\delta}{2}<\epsilon<\frac{1}{2}
,
0<\kappa<\frac{2\epsilon}{1-\delta}-1
and
c<c(\delta,\epsilon)
small enough, if the initial data
(w_{in},\theta_{in})
satisfies (
1.7
) then for any
T\geq T_{0}=\nu^{-\frac{1}{6}}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\nu\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon\left(t,D_{x},D_{y}\right)}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}(T_{0})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\nu^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\nu^{\frac{1}{4}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{3}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\nabla\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt\times\left(\nu^{\frac{1}{12}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 8.50012pt\left.+\nu^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\left(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\delta}{6}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}\|e^{c_{0}\nu\left|D_{x}\right|^{2}t^{3}}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}(L^{1}\cap L^{2})}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt\times\left(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}+\nu^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{2}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}.

Proof.

Taking the inner product of
\eqref{eq:nonlinear part}_{1}
with
\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}
, we have that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|\sqrt{\mathcal{M}}

\displaystyle e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\sqrt{\mathcal{M}}e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\nu\|\sqrt{\mathcal{M}}e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left(-\partial_{t}+k\partial_{\xi}\right)\mathcal{M}(k,\xi)e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(k)t}\langle k\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{k}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\left|\hat{\omega}^{\mathrm{NL}}(k,\xi)\right|^{2}dkd\xi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\Re\left(\left(u^{\mathrm{L}}+u^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\cdot\nabla\left(\omega^{\mathrm{L}}+\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)-\partial_{x}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\left\lvert\,\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\right.\right).

which gives

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|\sqrt{\mathcal{M}}e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\nu\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}}{16}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon\left(t,D_{x},D_{y}\right)}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left|\Re\left((u^{\mathrm{L}}+u^{\mathrm{NL}})\cdot\nabla(\omega^{\mathrm{L}}+\omega^{\mathrm{NL}})\big|\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\quad+2\|\sqrt{\mathcal{M}}e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{2}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\sqrt{\mathcal{M}}e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

Estimate of
u^{\mathrm{L}}\cdot\nabla\omega^{\mathrm{L}}
.
It is similar the estimate of
u^{\mathrm{L}}\cdot\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{L}}
and we get

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\left|\left(u^{\mathrm{L}}\cdot\nabla\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\big|\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Estimate of
u^{\mathrm{L}}\cdot\nabla\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}
.
Similarly, we have that

\displaystyle\left|\left(u^{\mathrm{L}}\cdot\nabla\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\big|\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{3}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\partial_{y}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\langle t\rangle^{-1}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\left(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\left.+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right).

Estimate of
u^{\mathrm{NL}}\cdot\nabla\omega^{\mathrm{L}}
.
Analogous to the estimates of
\theta
it concludes that

\displaystyle\left|\left(u^{\mathrm{NL}}\cdot\nabla\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\big|\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\delta}{6}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c_{0}\nu\left|D_{x}\right|^{2}t^{3}}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{1}\cap L^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}+\nu^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right).

Estimate of
u^{\mathrm{NL}}\cdot\nabla\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}
.
Using the same trick, we obtain that

\displaystyle\left|\left(u^{\mathrm{NL}}\cdot\nabla\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\big|\mathcal{M}e^{2c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{2}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}.

Summing up, we get that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|\sqrt{\mathcal{M}}e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\nu\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon\left(t,D_{x},D_{y}\right)}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{4}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\langle t\rangle^{-2}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{5}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{3}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\partial_{y}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\langle t\rangle^{-1}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt\times\left(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt\hskip 8.50012pt\left.+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\left(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\delta}{6}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c_{0}\nu\left|D_{x}\right|^{2}t^{3}}\langle D_{x},D_{y}+tD_{x}\rangle^{4}\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{2}\omega^{\mathrm{L}}\|_{L^{1}\cap L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt\times\left(\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}+\nu^{\frac{1}{6}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 17.00024pt\times\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}|D_{x}|^{\frac{2}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

which, combined with (
5.39
), completes the proof.
∎

At last, we present the proof of Proposition
2.5
.

Proof of Proposition
2.5
.

Let us designate
T
as the terminal point of the largest range
[T_{0},T]
such that the following hypothesis holds:

\displaystyle\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\nu\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon\left(t,D_{x},D_{y}\right)}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq 2C_{1}\nu^{1+4\delta},

\displaystyle\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\nu\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon\left(t,D_{x},D_{y}\right)}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\leq 2C_{1}^{\prime}\nu^{\frac{5}{3}+6\delta}.

By Proposition
5.3
, Proposition
2.2
and Corollary
2.4
, we get that

\displaystyle\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\nu\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon\left(t,D_{x},D_{y}\right)}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\nu^{\frac{4}{3}+4\delta}+C_{2}\left[\nu^{\frac{1}{6}}\nu^{\frac{1}{2}+2\delta}\nu^{\frac{2}{3}+2\delta}+\nu^{\frac{1}{4}}\nu^{\frac{1}{2}+2\delta}\nu^{\frac{1}{3}+\delta}\nu^{2\delta}+(\nu^{\frac{1}{2}+2\delta}+\nu^{\frac{1}{3}+2\delta})\nu^{\frac{1}{6}+\delta}\nu^{\frac{1}{2}+2\delta}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad+\nu^{\frac{1}{3}+\delta}(\nu^{\frac{1}{12}}\nu^{\frac{1}{2}+2\delta}\nu^{\frac{1}{3}+2\delta}+\nu^{\frac{1}{6}}\nu^{\frac{3}{2}(\frac{1}{3}+2\delta)}\nu^{\frac{1}{2}(2\delta)})+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\delta}{6}}\nu^{\frac{1}{2}+2\delta}\nu^{\frac{1}{3}+\delta}(\nu^{\frac{1}{2}+2\delta}+\nu^{\frac{1}{6}}\nu^{\frac{1}{3}+2\delta})\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad+\nu^{\frac{3}{2}(\frac{1}{3}+2\delta)}\nu^{\frac{1}{2}+2\delta}\nu^{\frac{1}{2}(2\delta)}+\nu^{\frac{1}{2}+2\delta}\nu^{\frac{1}{2}+2\delta}\nu^{2\delta}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\nu^{2(\frac{2}{3}+3\delta)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\nu^{\frac{4}{3}+4\delta}+C_{2}\nu^{1+4\delta}\left(\nu^{\frac{1}{6}}+\nu^{\frac{1}{12}+\delta}+\nu^{\frac{5}{6}\delta}+\nu^{\frac{1}{4}+\delta}\right)\leq(C_{1}+C_{2}\nu^{\frac{5}{6}\delta})\nu^{1+4\delta}.

For
\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}
, using Proposition
5.1
, Proposition
2.2
and Corollary
2.4
, we have that

\displaystyle\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{\infty}L^{2}}^{2}+\nu\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\nabla\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\left|D_{x}\right|^{\frac{1}{3}}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\sqrt{\Upsilon\left(t,D_{x},D_{y}\right)}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},T]}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}^{\prime}\nu^{\frac{5}{3}+6\delta}+C_{2}^{\prime}\left[\nu^{\frac{1}{6}}\nu^{\frac{5}{6}+3\delta}\nu^{\frac{1}{3}+\delta}\nu^{\frac{2}{3}+2\delta}+\nu^{\frac{1}{4}}\nu^{\frac{5}{6}+3\delta}\nu^{\frac{1}{3}+\delta}\nu^{\frac{1}{3}+3\delta}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad+\nu^{\frac{1}{3}+\delta}(\nu^{\frac{1}{12}}\nu^{\frac{5}{6}+3\delta}\nu^{\frac{2}{3}+3\delta}+\nu^{\frac{1}{6}}\nu^{\frac{3}{2}(\frac{2}{3}+3\delta)}\nu^{\frac{1}{2}(\frac{1}{3}+3\delta)})\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad+(\nu^{\frac{5}{6}+3\delta}\nu^{\frac{1}{2}+2\delta}+\nu^{\frac{2}{3}+3\delta}\nu^{\frac{1}{2}+2\delta})\nu^{\frac{1}{2}+2\delta}+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\delta}{6}}\nu^{\frac{1}{2}+2\delta}\nu^{\frac{2}{3}+2\delta}(\nu^{\frac{5}{6}+3\delta}+\nu^{\frac{1}{6}}\nu^{\frac{2}{3}+3\delta})\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad+\nu^{\frac{3}{2}(\frac{2}{3}+3\delta)}\nu^{\frac{1}{2}+2\delta}\nu^{\frac{1}{2}(\frac{1}{3}+3\delta)}+\nu^{\frac{5}{6}+3\delta}\nu^{\frac{1}{2}+2\delta}\nu^{\frac{1}{3}+3\delta}+\nu^{2(\frac{2}{3}+3\delta)}\nu^{\frac{1}{2}+2\delta}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}^{\prime}\nu^{\frac{5}{3}+6\delta}+C_{2}^{\prime}\nu^{\frac{5}{3}+6\delta}\left(\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}+\nu^{\frac{1}{12}+\delta}+\nu^{\frac{1}{4}+\delta}+\nu^{\delta}+\nu^{\frac{1}{3}+2\delta}+\nu^{\frac{1}{6}+2\delta}+\nu^{\frac{5}{6}\delta}+\nu^{2\delta}+\nu^{\frac{1}{6}+2\delta}\right)

\displaystyle\leq(C_{1}^{\prime}+C_{2}^{\prime}\nu^{\frac{5}{6}\delta})\nu^{\frac{5}{3}+6\delta}.

Choose
0<\nu_{2}<1
sufficiently small such that for all
0<\nu<\nu_{2}
,

\nu^{\frac{5}{6}\delta}\max\{C_{2},C_{2}^{\prime}\}\leq\frac{1}{2}\min\{C_{1},C_{1}^{\prime}\}.

Then, applying a standard bootstrap argument, we deduce that
T=+\infty
and

\displaystyle\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(D_{x})t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\tfrac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\omega^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[T_{0},+\infty]}L^{2}}+\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda\left(D_{x}\right)t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\frac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}\nabla\phi^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L_{[T_{0},+\infty]}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\nu^{-\frac{1}{3}-\delta}\|e^{c\nu^{\frac{1}{3}}\lambda(D_{x})t}\langle D_{x}\rangle\langle\tfrac{1}{D_{x}}\rangle^{\epsilon}\langle D_{x}\rangle^{\frac{1}{3}}\theta^{\mathrm{NL}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[T_{0},+\infty]}L^{2}}\lesssim\nu^{\frac{1}{2}+2\delta},

which completes the proof.
∎
